# Further metadata with previously attempted books filtered — 2026-11-02

Completed **250 additional previously missing numeric values**: 0 total-page counts, 250 Amazon rating aggregates, and 0 reference-page counts. 250 books updated; 134 score totals changed.

## Evidence and confidence

The primary assistant checked source identity, explicit numeric evidence, scope, and arithmetic. OpenRouter workers used Mistral Nemo for research and Mistral Small 24B for a separate evidence audit. Source text, not worker-generated numbers, determines acceptance. Metadata confidence is moderate. Amazon counts describe one matched product snapshot; the original indexed snapshot date is unknown. Counts are not guaranteed live and are not sums across products or written-review totals. Print counts identify a print edition. Reference counts use explicit inclusive bibliographical ranges, including catalogued pagination for digital editions where stated. Ratios across different or unconfirmed editions are low-confidence estimates. Unknown inputs retain their prescribed zero-bonus fallback.

Content assessments, eligibility, source memberships, and purchase links are retained. Only missing metadata and its resulting O15/RD scores change. [External audit resolutions](../data/batch250_261102/audit-resolutions.json) explain the primary assistant’s decisions on model flags.

## Cost and reusable findings

Saved successful OpenRouter responses report **$0.830721** in usage, including experiments and audit calls. This is recorded API usage, not an invoice. [Machine-readable cost and yield results](../data/batch250_261102/cost-learning.json) record the comparison.

Mistral Nemo with Parallel fast search, ten results and one-token research replies remains the base setup. This round tested two title-and-author clauses joined by OR in one search, retaining site:amazon.com and the phrase global ratings. The primary assistant reviewed the returned source annotations. Mistral Small 24B audited all 250 candidates in groups of five.

In the paired 20-book pilot, individual searches cost $0.021257 and found 7 verified target books; paired searches cost $0.010709 and found 5. Cost per verified target fell from $0.003037 to $0.002142, a 29.5% reduction. Target coverage fell too. We adopted paired searches for broad collection, retaining individual searches for unresolved priority books. This small sample does not establish equal recall or a universal saving. Only matches to the nominated work with explicit rating evidence count; wrong-author search hits are excluded.

There were 740 paired calls covering 1480 distinct book IDs and 20 individual pilot calls. The queue excluded 1954 previously attempted IDs. One accepted value reused old evidence. Secondary results were matched to their actual catalog works. Future attempt tracking must record both IDs in each paired call. No page or reference-page counts were accepted.

Recorded usage was $0.830721, or $0.003323 per accepted field: 14.4% below the preceding 250-value batch. Different books and cache availability confound that full-batch comparison. The paired pilot gives narrower supporting evidence for the method. Eight early audit calls were rate-limited; they were retried after most research finished. Audit concurrency remained two. Field-specific instructions allowed Kindle ratings and confined print-edition constraints to page-count fields. Remaining audit flags were resolved from the saved sources; no model output alone established a value.

Use cache first, pair two books per broad search, preserve title and author, and remap useful secondary results. Keep individual queries for coverage recovery. Source review and a separate audit remain necessary. Confidence is high for recorded cost arithmetic and moderate for metadata and generalizing the pilot.

## Verification

Published validation confirmed exactly 250 previously missing numeric values, unchanged existing counts, source memberships and raw content assessments, and correct score arithmetic. All accepted values received an external audit; ten remaining flags were resolved from the source. One abridged Walt Disney edition was rejected and replaced with Poking a Dead Frog; its five-value audit group was rerun. The superseded audit remains saved and its cost is included. No Amazon product was counted twice. The site build and all 21 tests passed.
